At inference, Visual Atmosphere Steering (VAS) injects atmosphere directions into hidden states, Semantic Affective Steering (SAS) isolates a separately scalable prompt residual for semantic cues, and Temporal Affective Steering (TAS) interpolates endpoint residual fields across denoising and video time. On Wan2.2, VAS improves target-emotion alignment by 19% while reducing a temporal-fluctuation proxy by 48%; SAS improves target-emotion alignment by 37% and increases detected affect-bearing cues by 36%; and TAS improves transition monotonicity by 15% over the strongest baseline. EmoWorld is evaluated across 27 emotion categories in text-to-video and image-to-video settings, demonstrates portability across multiple Video-DiT backbones, and supports camera-conditioned composition without updating generator parameters.

</details>


### [102] [Depth-Guided Video Object Counting in Crowded Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06236)
*Yuanjing Xu,Xinyan Liu,Weidong Chen,Zixuan Zou,Linhao Zhang,Zhuangzhe Meng,Antoni B. Chan,Weigang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出深度引导检测器（DG-Det）用于拥挤场景下的视频目标计数，通过融合深度信息与多尺度RGB-D交叉注意力及显式遮挡预测来增强空间理解，并引入统一去重框架消除跨帧冗余计数。此外发布了含深度信息的多类别RGB-D视频计数数据集，相比现有基线MAE降低62.01%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频目标计数方法依赖RGB信息，在拥挤和遮挡场景下判别能力有限，难以鲁棒地基于文本或视觉提示对目标类别进行准确计数。

Method: 提出Depth-Guided Detector（DG-Det），通过整合深度线索与多尺度RGB-D交叉注意力机制以及显式遮挡预测来提升空间理解；同时设计统一的去重框架以消除跨帧的冗余计数。

Result: 相比现有基线，MAE降低62.01%，RMSE也取得一致的改进；并发布了新的RGB-D视频目标计数数据集供后续研究使用。

Conclusion: 深度信息能够显著增强拥挤与遮挡场景下的视频目标计数鲁棒性，RGB-D融合策略和去重框架是有效手段，代码与数据集已开源以促进该领域发展。

Abstract: Our primary objective is to advance video object counting in crowded scenes, aiming to robustly count all instances of a target category based on given text or visual prompts. Existing methods rely on RGB information, limiting their discriminative ability in crowded and occluded conditions. To address this, we propose a Depth-Guided Detector (DG-Det) along with a general post-processing pipeline. By integrating depth cues with multi-scale RGB-D cross-attention and explicit occlusion prediction, our method enhances spatial understanding and achieves robust detection in crowded and occluded scenes. Furthermore, we introduce a unified de-duplication framework to eliminate cross-frame redundant counting. To facilitate future research, we also release a new RGB-D Video Object Counting dataset featuring depth information and multiple object categories persequence. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves a 62.01\% reduction in MAE compared to existing baselines, and also produces consistent improvements in RMSE. We provide the source code at https://github.com/streamer-AP/DG-Net and the dataset at https://huggingface.co/datasets/aerospace123/RGBD-VideoCount.

</details>


### [103] [PRISM: Distribution-Gated Flow Matching for Controllable Unpaired Image Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06240)
*Elad Yoshai,Natan T. Shaked*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PRISM是一个基于流匹配的无GAN非配对图像翻译框架，通过学习到的逐特征门控替代全局控制，在保留目标一致性特征的同时自由转化不匹配特征，在五个自然与生物医学基准上取得了最优或接近最优的FID/KID效果，并支持推理时局部覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的非配对图像翻译方法使用单一的全局噪声或引导值来控制内容保留，无法在空间上区分需要保留的内容与需要改变的外观，导致保留与转化之间的平衡难以精确控制。

Method: PRISM提出了一个无GAN的流匹配框架，核心是学习一个逐特征门的机制：门控的空间先验由每个源特征到目标特征分布的标准化距离导出，距离远（与目标不一致）的特征被释放、距离近（与目标一致）的特征被保留。该门控同时控制两个环节：(1) 初始化——混合真实源潜变量与任务匹配的扰动（内容锚定AdaIN用于保结构翻译，部分锚定用于变结构翻译）；(2) ODE积分过程中的传输时序。推理时门控可被文本或检测器局部覆盖而无需重新训练。

Result: 在五个基准（AFHQ cat->dog、CelebA-HQ外观翻译、day->night重光照、虚拟染色、乳腺癌冷冻->永久组织病理）的共享同划分协议下，PRISM在四个基准上取得最优Inception FID和KID，在第五个上取得有竞争力的结果；在组织病理任务上核计数比最接近理想值，体现了目标真实感与结构保留之间的良好平衡。

Conclusion: 通过逐特征门控取代全局控制，PRISM在非配对图像翻译中实现了对内容保留与外观改变的空间可控分离，在多个领域（含生物医学）均验证了其有效性和结构保留能力，并支持无需重训的推理时局部干预。

Abstract: Unpaired image-to-image translation must decide, per image, what to change and what to preserve without paired supervision. Many diffusion-based unpaired translators control preservation through a single global noise or guidance value applied across the image, which cannot separate content to keep from appearance to change. We present PRISM, a GAN-free flow-matching framework that replaces this global control with a learned per-feature gate. The gate's spatial prior is derived from each source feature's standardized distance to the target feature distribution, so features far from the target are freed while target-consistent features are preserved. The same gate controls both the initialization, which mixes the real source latent with a task-matched corruption, and the transport timing during Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE) integration. The corruption is matched to the task, content-anchored (AdaIN) for structure-preserving translation and partially anchored for structure-changing translation, and the gate can be overridden locally at inference from text or a detector without retraining, preserving important structures of the original image while still generating realistic results. We evaluate PRISM on five natural and biomedical benchmarks (AFHQ cat->dog, CelebA-HQ appearance translation, day->night relighting, virtual staining, and breast frozen->permanent histopathology). Among the evaluated methods under a shared same-split protocol, PRISM attains the best Inception FID and KID on four benchmarks and a competitive result on the fifth, and on histopathology yields the nuclei-count ratio closest to the ideal, supporting a favorable balance between target realism and structural preservation.

</details>


### [104] [Toward Deployable Bangla Sign Language Recognition with Expert-Validated Data and a Lightweight Attention-Based Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06252)
*Saad Ahmed,Md Khalid Syfullaha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RSBdSL38数据集（10,874张经专家验证的孟加拉手语图片，涵盖38种手语手势）和一个轻量级注意力卷积网络（298,470参数），从零训练达到96.37%准确率，仅比最佳ImageNet预训练模型低1.08个百分点，但参数减少8.5-68倍，计算量减少1.3-21.7倍。量化后仅0.48 MB，可在普通智能手机上以3.98 ms/张的速度运行，将基准精度转化为可部署的无障碍应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孟加拉聋哑人群主要通过孟加拉手语（BdSL）交流，自动识别可扩展教育和服务的可及性。现有系统依赖非专家验证的受控数据集，且使用不适合端侧部署的重型预训练骨干网络，缺乏轻量、可靠的解决方案。

Method: 构建RSBdSL38数据集（从孟加拉三所特教学校采集真实手语者图片，覆盖全部38种手语表示字母表51个字母）。提出轻量级注意力卷积网络，包含分组瓶颈残差块、通道与空间注意力、多尺度深度可分离手部特征块、双池化和Swish激活函数，参数量仅298,470。与9种ImageNet预训练高效架构进行相同协议对比，并做消融研究（逐阶段移除）、Grad-CAM可解释性验证、跨人划分测试和量化部署评估。

Result: RSBdSL38上从零训练达到96.37%准确率（5个种子平均95.72%±0.54%），仅比最佳预训练架构低1.08个百分点；在6个公共BdSL基准上重训达到92.95-98.33%，合并语料97.04%，BdSL-38零样本76.25%；移除任一架构阶段损失7.61-89.30个点（训练配方最多3.17）；留出6/36手语者的跨人划分准确率85.18%；量化后0.48 MB，手机端15.5 MB内存内以3.98 ms/张运行。

Conclusion: RSBdSL38数据集和从零训练的轻量模型将基准准确率转化为可部署的端侧无障碍方案，以极低的预训练骨干成本实现同等精度，数据集、代码和模型均已开源。

Abstract: Deaf and hard-of-hearing people in Bangladesh communicate mainly through Bangla Sign Language (BdSL). Automatic BdSL recognition on personal devices could widen access to education and services. Existing systems use controlled-setting datasets without expert verification and heavyweight pretrained backbones unsuited to on-device use. We introduce RSBdSL38, 10,874 expert-validated images spanning all 38 BdSL hand signs, representing the 51 letters of the Bangla alphabet, recorded from real signers at three special-needs schools across Bangladesh. We propose a lightweight attention based convolutional network of 298,470 parameters, built from grouped bottleneck residual blocks, channel and spatial attention, a multi-scale depthwise hand-feature block, dual pooling, and Swish activations. Trained from scratch, it attains 96.37% accuracy (95.72% +- 0.54% over five seeds), within 1.08 percentage points of the best of nine ImageNet-pretrained efficient architectures under an identical protocol, using 8.5 to 68x fewer parameters and 1.3 to 21.7x fewer MACs. Retrained, it reaches 92.95 to 98.33% on six public BdSL benchmarks, 97.04% on a merged corpus, and 76.25% zero-shot on BdSL-38. Removing any architectural stage costs 7.61 to 89.30 points, against at most 3.17 for the training recipe. Grad-CAM with deletion-insertion and weight-randomization checks confirms that predictions follow the signing hand. A signer-independent split holding out 6 of 36 signers yields 85.18%. Quantized to 0.48 MB, it runs at 3.98 ms per image within a 15.5 MB footprint on a commodity smartphone. Together, RSBdSL38 and our from-scratch model turn benchmark accuracy into deployable accessibility at a fraction of pretrained-backbone cost; dataset, code, and models are released.

</details>


### [105] [MASS: Multiplayer World Models with Authoritative Shared State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06257)
*Ziqi Cai,Siqi Yang,Yimu Wang,Zixian Gao,Yunheng Liu,Shuchen Weng,Erwin Wu,Kaipeng Zhang,Boxin Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MAS（Multiplayer world models with Authoritative Shared State）框架，通过将世界动态与视图渲染显式解耦，实现可扩展且跨视图一致的多玩家世界模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型在多玩家环境中存在缺陷：世界状态与视图相关的视觉潜变量纠缠在一起，导致计算冗余、视图不一致以及可扩展性差。

Method: 受多人游戏架构启发，MAS将世界动态与视图渲染解耦：学习型Logic Engine从联合动作推进全局权威类型化状态（无需手写转移函数），作为唯一的循环记忆与同步参考；学习型Rendering Engine从共享状态按需生成任意相机的独立且一致的视图。

Result: 在匹配的多玩家Snake基准上，MAS相比最先进的多视图基线实现了更优的状态准确度和更低的跨视图不一致性；可推进包含1,024个并发玩家、10,000个循环步骤的预测世界。

Conclusion: 显式的权威状态建模为可扩展且一致的多智能体世界模拟提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Current video world models struggle in multiplayer environments because they entangle world state with view-dependent visual latents, leading to redundant compute, view inconsistencies, and poor scalability. We propose MAS (Multiplayer world models with Authoritative Shared State) to resolve this limitation. Inspired by multiplayer game architectures, MAS disentangles world dynamics and view rendering. A learned Logic Engine advances a global, authoritative typed state from joint actions without any hand-written transition function, acting as the sole recurrent memory and synchronization reference. From this shared state, a learned Rendering Engine generates independent and consistent views for any requested camera on demand. This explicit disentangling allows MAS to achieve superior state accuracy and lower cross-view inconsistency compared to state-of-the-art multi-view baselines on a matched multiplayer Snake benchmark. It advances predicted worlds with 1,024 concurrent players for 10,000 recurrent steps. Our results show that explicit, authoritative state modeling provides a practical foundation for scalable and consistent multi-agent world simulation.

</details>


### [106] [TLNM: Externally Validated Tooth Detection, Numbering and Segmentation from Smartphone Photographs Using Mask R-CNN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06275)
*Arash Nedaei,Henna Tiensuu,Elina Väyrynen,Saujanya Karki,Jaakko Suutala*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于智能手机照片的牙齿定位与编号模型，使用定制化的Mask R-CNN管道，结合掩膜灰度世界白平衡算法和解剖约束检测层，在1,272张标注的智能手机图像上训练。内部测试集上实例掩膜AP@50达0.818，外部数据集上达0.901，且训练稳定性高（标准差0.009）。该管道以开源容器化API形式提供，为远程牙科筛查提供了可扩展、低成本的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球数十亿人受口腔健康问题困扰，但临床级影像设备成本高、可及性差，阻碍了公众自我筛查和预防性口腔保健。现有研究依赖临床级X光片或口内相机图像，普通公众无法获得这些资源，因此亟需一种基于日常智能手机照片的牙齿定位与编号方法。

Method: 开发了一个定制化Mask R-CNN管道：(1) 在1,272张标注的智能手机图像上训练，用于牙齿定位和编号；(2) 引入掩膜灰度世界白平衡算法以缓解患者生成健康数据中的人工色偏；(3) 加入解剖约束检测层以强制结构有效性并抑制假阳性。评估分为四个阶段：内部留出测试、独立外部测试、消融研究及十折训练稳定性分析。

Result: 内部测试集上：实例掩膜AP@50为0.818，类感知PQ为0.780，操作F1为0.884；十次运行训练稳定性标准差仅0.009。外部数据集上（不同人群、传感器和采集协议）：实例掩膜AP@50为0.901，类感知PQ为0.832，操作F1为0.928。尽管存在域差异，外部性能甚至优于内部性能。

Conclusion: 消费级智能手机图像足以支持自动化的牙齿级解剖映射，为资源受限环境中的远程筛查和远程牙科提供了可扩展、潜在低成本的解决方案。该推理管道已作为开源容器化API公开可用。

Abstract: Oral health issues affect billions globally, but the cost and limited access to professional dental care hinder preventive oral healthcare. Research relies on clinical-grade radiographs or intraoral camera images, unavailable for public self-screening. This study introduces a tooth localisation and numbering model for smartphone photographs. We developed a customised Mask Region-based Convolutional Neural Network (Mask R-CNN) pipeline trained on 1,272 annotated smartphone images. To address variability in patient-generated health data, the pipeline incorporates two domain-informed mechanisms: a masked gray-world white-balancing algorithm to mitigate artificial colour casts and an anatomically constrained detection layer to enforce structural validity and suppress false positives. Evaluation comprised four stages: internal held-out testing, independent external testing, a descriptive ablation study, and fold-based training stability analysis using the same internal test set. On the internal test set, the model achieved an instance-mask AP@50 of 0.818, class-aware PQ of 0.780, and operational F1 of 0.884. Training stability showed limited between-model variation: across ten runs, instance-mask AP@50 had a standard deviation of 0.009. On the external dataset, the model achieved an instance-mask AP@50 of 0.901, class-aware PQ of 0.832, and operational F1 of 0.928 despite differences in population, sensors, and acquisition protocols. The inference pipeline is available as an open-source, containerised API. These results demonstrate that consumer-grade smartphone imagery can support automated tooth-level anatomical mapping, offering a scalable, potentially low-cost foundation for remote screening and tele-dentistry in resource-constrained environments.

</details>


### [107] [UQ-Loc: Uncertainty-Aware LiDAR Scene Coordinate Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06307)
*Jacek Komorowski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UQ-Loc是一个基于LiDAR的场景坐标回归（SCR）方法，通过引入各向异性高斯协方差头来预测每个体素的全3x3正定协方差矩阵，从而捕获认知不确定性。该方法使用NLL损失加kNN空间平滑正则化进行训练，并采用改进的SC2-PCR求解器（含不确定性加权种子评分和马氏距离内点检验）进行推理，最终在提升6-DoF定位精度的同时产生校准良好的协方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LiDAR场景坐标回归方法仅产生确定性预测，丢弃了可提高鲁棒性和下游决策质量的偶然不确定性（aleatoric uncertainty）信息。

Method: 1) 扩展LightLoc架构，新增各向异性高斯协方差头，预测每个体素的完整3x3正定协方差矩阵；2) 使用负对数似然（NLL）损失并附加kNN空间平滑正则化项；3) 推理阶段采用修改的SC2-PCR求解器，包括不确定性加权种子评分和马氏距离内点检验。

Result: 实验表明UQ-Loc在6-DoF定位精度上取得一致性提升，同时生成良好校准的协方差（通过ECE指标验证）。

Conclusion: 将不确定性量化为预测的补充维度，不仅能校准预测质量，还能通过不确定性引导的求解器改进6-DoF定位精度，为LiDAR定位中的不确定性感知决策提供了有效方案。

Abstract: LiDAR-based Scene Coordinate Regression (SCR) maps point clouds directly to 3D scene coordinates, enabling precise 6-DoF localisation without explicit map retrieval. However, existing methods produce deterministic predictions, discarding aleatoric uncertainty that could improve robustness and downstream decision-making. We present UQ-Loc, which extends the LightLoc architecture with an anisotropic Gaussian covariance head that predicts a full 3x3 positive-definite covariance matrix per voxel. Training uses a Negative Log-Likelihood (NLL) loss augmented with a kNN-based spatial smoothness regulariser, while inference employs a modified SC2-PCR solver with uncertainty-weighted seed scoring and a Mahalanobis-distance inlier test. We adopt Expected Calibration Error (ECE) as a principled metric for evaluating the quality of the predicted uncertainty. Experiments demonstrate that UQ-Loc achieves consistent improvement in 6-DoF localization accuracy while producing well-calibrated covariances.

</details>


### [108] [Does FLAIR super-resolution erase or hallucinate small white-matter lesions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06311)
*Zahra Khodakarami,Yue Li,Pulkit Khandelwal,John Detre,Sandhitsu Das,Christopher Brown,David Wolk,Paul Yushkevich*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了在FLAIR影像上先进行超分辨率重建再执行白质高信号（WMH）分割的影响，发现超分辨率的主要效应是抹除小的真实病灶而非幻觉生成病灶，且该效应随切层厚度增加而加剧；多对比度隐式神经表示（INR）与三次插值效果相当，而单对比度自监督模型ECLARE在保留小病灶信号方面表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床FLAIR扫描通常采用厚切片采集，导致面外分辨率较低。超分辨率技术虽广泛用于恢复各向同性体积，但其在WMH分割之前应用是否会保留病灶内容（即不抹除真实小病灶、不幻觉生成不存在的病灶）仍是未知问题。

Method: 使用ADNI队列中29例个体的1mm各向同性高分辨率FLAIR扫描，由专家手动分割WMH；将高分辨率数据降质模拟为3mm和5mm的面外采集。采用多对比度隐式神经表示（INR）、单对比度自监督模型ECLARE和三次插值三种方法进行超分辨率重建。在四种WMH分割方法中选用对HR小病灶最敏感的MARS-WMH进行逐病灶分析，评价指标包括检测灵敏度、抹除率（HR检测到的病灶在重建后丢失的比例）和幻觉率（预测成分在手动和HR分割中均不存在的比例）。

Result: 超分辨率的主要效应是抹除小的真实病灶而非生成幻觉病灶，且该效应随切片厚度增加而加剧；所有重建方法相对于原始厚切片均改善了病灶检测。ECLARE在两种厚度下均最好地保留了小病灶信号，而INR的表现与三次插值相当。

Conclusion: 在FLAIR超分辨率后执行WMH分割时，小病灶的抹除是主要风险而非幻觉，不同超分辨率方法在病灶保留能力上存在显著差异，ECLARE表现最优，这提示在实际临床流程中选择合适的SR方法对WMH分割至关重要。

Abstract: White matter hyperintensities (WMH), bright regions on Fluid-attenuated Inversion Recovery (FLAIR) scans are associated with cerebrovascular pathology and neurodegeneration. FLAIR is usually acquired with thick slices in clinical settings, giving it poor through-plane resolution. Super-resolution (SR) is a widely used method for recovering an isotropic volume from an anisotropic scan. Yet whether applying it prior to WMH segmentation preserves lesion content remains unknown: a model may erase small real lesions or hallucinate absent ones. We used 1-mm isotropic high-resolution (HR) FLAIR scans from 29 individuals in the ADNI cohort, each manually segmented for WMH by an expert. Then, we degraded each to simulated 3 and 5 mm through-plane acquisitions. Multi-contrast implicit neural representation (INR), a single-contrast self-supervised model (ECLARE), and cubic interpolation were used to upsample them onto the HR grid. WMH segmentation from a simulated thick slice and the original HR FLAIR set the floor and ceiling, respectively, for the per-lesion analysis. Of four WMH segmentation methods (WMH-SynthSeg, segcsvd, MARS-WMH, TrUE-Net), we ran the analysis under the most sensitive one to small lesions on HR (MARS-WMH) with the evaluation metrics of detection sensitivity, erasure rate (HR-detected lesions lost after reconstruction), and hallucination rate (predicted components absent from both the manual and HR segmentation). The dominant effect of SR was erasure of small real lesions, not hallucination, and it increased with slice thickness, though every reconstruction still improved lesion detection over the raw thick slice. ECLARE recovered small lesion signal best at both thicknesses, while the INR was no better than cubic interpolation.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [109] [EdgeXpert: An Edge Device for Memory-Efficient LLM Inference with Mixture-of-Experts and Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05303)
*Sangwoo Ha,Hyunwoo Seo,Yurim Jo,Youngjin Moon,Hoi-Jun Yoo*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: EdgeXpert是一个软硬件协同设计的LLM加速器，解决了投机解码（Speculative Decoding）与混合专家（MoE）结合时的兼容性问题，通过预填充阶段的提示级专家复用和解码阶段的深度感知专家合并技术，实现了高达56.3%的延迟降低和44.1%的能耗降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端侧LLM部署面临外部内存访问（EMA）瓶颈，主要出现在前馈网络（FFN）层。投机解码和MoE分别是减少解码阶段数量和降低单阶段开销的有效方案，但两者结合时存在不兼容问题，需要一个统一的解决方案来同时发挥两者的优势。

Method: EdgeXpert采用两阶段协同设计：(1) 预填充阶段，通过提示级专家复用（prompt-wise expert reuse）将路由从逐token独立选择重构为提示级专家集合共享，利用轻量级编码器识别重要token并构建共享专家集，对次要token减少专家预算以降低EMA开销；(2) 解码阶段，通过深度感知专家合并（depth-aware expert coalescing）利用同深度候选token的上下文相似性和互斥性，仅加载关键通道而非全部所需通道的并集，并通过计算校准恢复精度而无需额外内存访问。

Result: 在三星28nm工艺、800MHz频率下综合实现，EdgeXpert相比先前工作实现了最高56.3%的延迟降低和44.1%的能耗降低，同时保持接近基线的精度。

Conclusion: EdgeXpert通过软硬件协同设计成功解决了投机解码与MoE之间的不兼容问题，为端侧LLM高效部署提供了一种兼顾延迟、能耗和精度的可行方案。

Abstract: On-device deployment of Large Language Models (LLMs) has become essential for personalized edge applications. A primary bottleneck is external memory access (EMA) in feed-forward network (FFN) layers. Speculative decoding and mixture-of-experts (MoE) are promising solutions. Speculative decoding reduces the number of decoding stages by generating multiple tokens per stage, and MoE minimizes per-stage cost through sparse expert activation. However, there is an incompatibility when combining these two techniques. We propose EdgeXpert, a software-hardware co-designed LLM accelerator that resolves this incompatibility. In the prefill stage, the prompt-wise expert reuse reformulates routing as prompt-level expert reuse rather than independent per-token expert selection. It identifies important tokens using a lightweight encoder, constructs a shared expert set from them, and routes less important tokens with a reduced expert budget to lower expert EMA. In the decode stage, depth-aware expert coalescing exploits the contextual similarity and mutual exclusivity of same-depth candidate tokens. Rather than loading the union of all required channels, EdgeXpert loads only salient channels and applies computational calibration to recover accuracy without additional memory access. Synthesized in Samsung 28nm technology at 800 MHz, EdgeXpert achieves up to 56.3% latency reduction and 44.1% energy reduction compared to prior works, while maintaining near-baseline accuracy.

</details>


### [110] [PowerScope: ML-based Intra-Cycle Power Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05339)
*Jayanth Balasubramanian,Sujay Pandit,Radha Vaidya,Anand Raghunathan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出PowerScope，这是首个基于机器学习的亚时钟周期（intra-cycle）功耗估计框架，仅依赖RTL仿真轨迹即可在推理时工作，无需后布局门级仿真和功耗分析。在多样化基准测试中，中位误差5.88%、平均误差9%，速度比商业后布局门级功耗估计快约80倍，并能可靠用于硅前功耗侧信道泄漏评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亚时钟周期分辨率下的功耗估计对电源传输网络设计、动态电压跌落分析和硅前功耗侧信道安全评估至关重要。现有商业后布局门级功耗分析工具计算开销大且难扩展；已有的ML功耗估计方法仅支持平均功耗或每周期功耗，无法满足亚时钟周期分辨率的估计需求。

Method: PowerScope是首个基于ML的亚时钟周期功耗估计框架。推理时仅使用RTL仿真轨迹作为输入，避免了每个工作负载都需要后布局门级仿真和功耗分析的流程。该方法通过ML模型直接学习从RTL活动信息到子时钟周期功耗的映射关系。

Result: 在多样化基准测试集上，与商业后布局门级功耗估计相比，PowerScope实现5.88%的中位绝对百分比误差和9%的平均绝对百分比误差，运行速度约快80倍。此外，其预测结果可可靠用于硅前功耗侧信道泄漏评估这一下游任务。

Conclusion: PowerScope证明了ML方法能够有效实现亚时钟周期分辨率的功耗估计，兼具高准确性和显著的加速优势，并能为功耗侧信道安全评估等下游应用提供可靠支持。

Abstract: Power estimation at sub-clock-cycle temporal resolutions is critical for tasks such as power delivery network (PDN) design, dynamic voltage droop analysis, and pre-silicon power side-channel security evaluation. Designers commonly rely on commercial post-layout gate-level power analysis tools for these tasks, but these flows are computationally expensive and scale poorly with design size and workload length. Machine learning (ML)-based power estimation frameworks have shown promise in accelerating power estimation, but prior efforts only address average power or per-cycle power estimation. We propose PowerScope, the first ML-based intra-cycle power estimation framework. PowerScope operates purely on RTL simulation traces at inference time, eliminating the need for post-layout gate-level simulation and power analysis per workload. Across a diverse benchmark suite, PowerScope achieves 5.88% median and 9% mean absolute percentage error compared to commercial post-layout gate-level power estimates while running ~80x faster. We further demonstrate that PowerScope's predictions can be reliably used for the downstream task of pre-silicon power side-channel leakage assessment.

</details>


### [111] [PLoRA: An NDP-Enhanced Pooled-Memory System for Cost-Efficient Multi-LoRA Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05483)
*Zhongkai Yu,Ohm Rishabh Venkatachalam,Zheng Wang,Yikai Li,Yichen Lin,Zihao Yu,Yuke Wang,Liu Liu,Xulong Tang,Shuyi Pei,Yangwook Kang,Yufei Ding*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: PLoRA是一个基于近数据处理（NDP）增强的池化内存系统，用于高效的多LoRA服务。它通过仅将缩减结果返回链路的方式，将适配器和KV缓存保留在内存池中，实现了在H100上服务1000个适配器时平均解码延迟比S-LoRA低6.6倍的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多LoRA服务将单个基础模型转变为数千个专用变体，但现有系统必须通过PCIe从CPU DRAM分阶段加载适配器，每次访问都产生内核停顿和主机运行的拷贝开销，且容量受限于主板DIMM插槽。而CXL和NVLink等内存语义织物正汇聚于池化内存，如何在这种硬件上服务多LoRA工作负载仍未被探索。

Method: PLoRA采用NDP增强的池化内存系统：将适配器和KV缓存保存在池中，通过GPU自身的load/store操作的读计算接口仅返回缩减结果。其上构建GPU内存管理系统，针对每个适配器从四种LoRA和两种注意力执行策略中选择，并由链路参数化成本模型指导缓存GPU内存中性能最关键的字节。

Result: 在单块H100上服务1000个适配器，PLoRA在所有模型和工作负载上取得最低解码延迟，平均比真实机器上的S-LoRA低6.6倍，且仅增加不到3.4%的设备面积。吞吐量在短上下文下于32 GB/s处饱和（仅为CXL 3.1的四分之一），使得链路不再是瓶颈。设计可从CXL级无缝运行到NVLink级织物，剩余带宽用于购买池化容量而非速度。

Conclusion: PLoRA证明通过NDP增强的池化内存系统，可以将适配器流量与张量并行进行分片，使单GPU需求从7B下降到1.2T部署规模，从而有效解决多LoRA服务的容量和带宽瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Multi-LoRA serving is how one base model becomes thousands of specialized variants, one adapter per user, task, or agent, and the deployments can hold 1000-plus adapters. Serving them is hard because the workload inverts what GPUs provide: terabytes of memory against only tens of TFLOPS, and because every published system stages its adapters from CPU DRAM over PCIe, where each access pays a kernel stop and a host-run copy and capacity ends at the motherboard's DIMM slots. Meanwhile, memory-semantic fabrics such as CXL and NVLink are converging on pooled memory that an accelerator addresses with its own loads and stores, and near-data processing (NDP) can place compute beside the pooled data. How to serve multi-LoRA workloads on such hardware remains unexplored.
  This paper introduces PLoRA, an NDP-enhanced pooled-memory system for cost-efficient multi-LoRA serving. PLoRA keeps adapters and KV cache in the pool and returns only reduced results over the link, through a read-compute interface the GPU drives with its own loads and stores. Above this architecture, a GPU memory management system picks among four LoRA and two attention execution strategies for each adapter and caches the most performance-critical bytes in GPU memory, guided by a link-parameterized cost model. On one H100 serving 1000 adapters, PLoRA attains the lowest decode latency on every model and workload we measure, averaging 6.6x below a real-machine S-LoRA at under 3.4% added device area. The link itself stops mattering: throughput saturates at 32 GB/s on short contexts, a quarter of CXL 3.1, and the verdict survives scale: per-GPU demand falls from 7B to a modeled 1.2T deployment once adapter traffic shards with the tensor parallelism. The design runs unchanged from CXL-class to NVLink-class fabrics, and surplus bandwidth buys pooled capacity rather than speed.

</details>


### [112] [Zero-Instruction Sensor Reads: Register-Mapped Peripherals and Hardware PWM on a Five-Stage Soft Processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05638)
*Nathanael Ren*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文通过一个应用驱动的软处理器特化案例研究，将轮式自平衡自行车的内部控制回路作为评估场景。设计了两种特化方式：将两个频繁访问的外设输入直接映射为架构寄存器状态，消除了专用读取指令；将四个周期性PWM通道卸载到硬件，免除了软件中的波形维护。实验显示最坏情况控制循环从91周期降至43周期，在20ms执行帧下提供了约7300倍和15000倍的余量，但作者指出该特化真正的价值在于指令计数和软件简洁性，而非实时确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在应用驱动下对软处理器进行特化的方法，通过将外设I/O直接映射到寄存器状态和硬件卸载周期性任务，来减少控制循环中的指令数和周期数，从而简化软件并提高性能。

Method: 在MIPS传统的自定义32位RISC核心基础上进行两种特化：(1) 将两个高频访问的外设输入直接映射为架构寄存器，由硬件每周期写入，通过寄存器文件的写端口结构独占访问而非仲裁；(2) 将四个周期性PWM通道卸载到硬件，由四个导出寄存器连续驱动，从软件中完全移除周期性驱动任务。报告了归档配置（91周期）和集成配置（43周期）两种部署。

Result: 控制循环中的全部十次传感器读取不需要专用指令和循环，可折叠到已执行的算术操作中，而内存映射方式需要每次快照显式加载，多花5条指令和周期。最坏情况循环从91周期降至43周期，在20ms执行帧下提供约7300倍和15000倍的余量。

Conclusion: 特化并非实时合规所必需，因为即使未特化的版本也能以极大余量满足截止时间。该特化设计的真正价值在于减少指令计数和提升软件简洁性，而非确定性——片内单周期I/O区域本身已提供了确定性。

Abstract: We present a case study in application-driven specialization of a five-stage soft processor, evaluated on the inner control loop of a reaction-wheel self-balancing bicycle. Starting from a custom 32-bit RISC core in the MIPS tradition, we specialize the design in two ways. First, two frequently accessed peripheral inputs are mapped directly into architectural register state, written every cycle by hardware and owned exclusively through the register file's write-port structure rather than by arbitration. Second, four periodic PWM channels are offloaded to hardware and driven continuously from four exported registers, removing periodic actuation from software entirely. Because peripheral values are addressable as ordinary register operands, all ten sensor reads in the control loop cost no dedicated instruction and no dedicated cycle, folding into arithmetic that executes anyway; the memory-mapped equivalent requires an explicit load per snapshot and costs five extra instructions and cycles. The actuation path likewise removes waveform maintenance from software entirely. We report two configurations, because the extensions and the single-cycle array multiplier they were deployed alongside are not present together in a single archived build: an archived configuration, whose worst-case loop is 91 cycles, and the integrated configuration matching the deployed system, at 43 cycles. Against a 20 ms actuation frame these are margins of roughly 7,300x and 15,000x. The deadline is met by so wide a margin in either case that the specialization was not necessary for real-time compliance; its value lies in instruction count and software simplicity, not in determinism, which an on-chip single-cycle I/O region already provides. The zero-instruction sensor read is independent of that choice: the multiplier cannot affect whether a peripheral read needs an instruction of its own.

</details>


### [113] [An Open-Source Power Measurement Platform for System-Level Semiconductor Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05888)
*Linus Bantel,Sarah Rottacker,Dirk Pflüger*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个基于Raspberry Pi、精密电流测量设备和微控制器DUT的紧凑型可扩展硬件平台，用于嵌入式设备的自动化系统级功耗测量，支持远程固件部署和测量结果检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确的功耗测量对于评估现代嵌入式及半导体系统的能效至关重要，但工业级半导体测试设备通常昂贵且难以集成到灵活的实验工作流中。

Method: 该平台集成Raspberry Pi控制器、精密电流测量设备和基于微控制器的被测设备（DUT），支持自动化固件部署、同步设备执行和高分辨率电流采集；通过轻量级HTTP接口实现远程固件上传和测量结果检索，使用开放的硬件组件和开源软件构建。

Result: 实现了一个低成本、可扩展的功耗测量平台，能够支持自动化基准测试和跨多种固件版本的可复现实验，适用于固件能耗分析和回归测试等场景。

Conclusion: 该平台凭借可获取的硬件组件和开源软件，适用于研究、原型开发和教学环境，为嵌入式系统功耗测量提供了一个低成本且可复现的替代方案。

Abstract: Accurate power measurement is not only essential for evaluating the energy efficiency of modern embedded and semiconductor systems, but power draw is an important proxy during stress testing. Industrial semiconductor test equipment, however, is often expensive and difficult to integrate into flexible experimental workflows.
  This paper presents a compact and extensible hardware platform for automated system-level power measurement of embedded devices. The proposed setup integrates a Raspberry Pi controller, a precision current measurement device, and a microcontroller-based Device-under-Test (DUT). The system supports automated firmware deployment, synchronized device execution, and high-resolution current acquisition.
  A lightweight HTTP-based interface allows remote clients to upload firmware binaries and retrieve measurement results. This design enables automated benchmarking and reproducible experiments across multiple firmware variants.
  The platform is implemented using accessible hardware components and open-source software tools, making it suitable for research, prototyping, and educational environments. We describe the architecture of the platform, the automated testing workflow, and potential application scenarios, including firmware energy profiling and regression testing.

</details>


### [114] [Automated Synthesis of Heterogeneous, Hierarchical, Scoped Coherence Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05965)
*Fletch Rydell,An Qi Zhang,Nicolai Oswald,Andres Goens,Vijay Nagarajan,Daniel Sorin*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出ShimGen，一个基于shim API的自动综合引擎，用于在异构、分层、基于作用域的缓存一致性协议之间自动生成接口（shim），支持SWMR和放宽访问模式。在两个案例中验证了其正确性优于手工设计，并展示了非SWMR全局协议的性能优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 处理器设计正趋向于异构、分层、带作用域的缓存一致性共享内存模型（如CXL和AMBA CHI作为全局协议，各集群有自己的协议）。手工设计集群协议与全局协议之间的shim接口既微妙又易出错；而自动综合方法通常做了诸如SWMR（单写者多读者）等简化假设，牺牲性能换取简单性和正确性保证。

Method: 提出了一个shim API——一种通用抽象，可将协议事务依据其语义一致性保证进行分类。基于该API构建自动化综合引擎ShimGen，能够自动组合同时包含SWMR和放宽访问的协议，并利用现代架构优化如作用域内存访问（scoped memory accesses）和惰性失效（lazy invalidation）。

Result: 两个案例研究：1) 与gem5中发布的AMD APU手工设计分层协议对比，ShimGen输出与其相似，但发现手工设计协议在一场景下未能维持复合一致性，而ShimGen的输出正确。2) 性能对比显示，采用非SWMR行为的全局协议比分层次严格SWMR全局协议具有性能优势。

Conclusion: ShimGen能够自动综合正确且高效的分层缓存一致性协议shim，不仅支持SWMR还支持非SWMR（放宽）访问模式，在正确性上优于手工设计，并在性能上展现了放宽访问的优势。

Abstract: Processor design is converging on a new model of cache-coherent shared memory characterized by heterogeneity, hierarchy, and scopes. Protocols like CXL or AMBA CHI are used as global protocols to combine multiple clusters, each with its own cluster-level coherence protocols. Manually designing shims to interface between these cluster and global protocols is subtle and error-prone. Automatic synthesis, on the other hand, makes simplifying assumptions like the single-writer multiple-reader (SWMR) invariant, that sacrifice performance for simplicity and guaranteed correctness.
  We present a shim API \textemdash a generic abstraction that enables us to classify protocol transactions by their semantic coherence guarantees. Our automated synthesis engine, ShimGen, uses this shim API to automatically compose protocols with both SWMR and relaxed accesses, taking advantage of modern architectural optimizations like scoped memory accesses and lazy invalidation. We demonstrate ShimGen's efficacy on two case studies. First, we compare its output to a manually-designed hierarchical protocol for the AMD APU released in gem5. ShimGen's output is similar to the existing protocol. However, we identify one scenario where the manually-designed protocol fails to uphold compound consistency, while ShimGen's does. Second, we compare the performance of a hierarchical protocol with a global protocol that exploits non-SWMR accesses to a hierarchical protocol with a strictly SWMR global protocol. The result shows the performance benefits of accommodating global protocols with non-SWMR behavior.

</details>


### [115] [Breaking Memory Bottlenecks in Quantum Control Systems for More Precise Experiments and Higher Throughput Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06318)
*Yicheng Guang,Neel Vora,Yilun Xu,Yueqi Chen,Gang Huang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: Ant-Q是一个面向量子控制系统的存储层次设计，通过将DRAM与BRAM集成、支持流水线化量子电路执行，打破了由片上BRAM容量有限和DRAM非确定性延迟导致的存储瓶颈，使电路加载和读出上行开销从执行时间的22.90%-1417.05%降至接近零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算日益受到关注，需要更精确的实验来推动量子器件发展，以及更高的电路吞吐量来验证更多领域应用。然而，量子控制系统层的存储瓶颈——片上BRAM容量有限和DRAM的非确定性延迟——阻碍了这些需求的实现。

Method: 提出Ant-Q，一种将DRAM与BRAM集成的存储层次设计，支持流水线化量子电路执行，同时确保电路间的确定性时序。该设计通过存储层次的优化，解决BRAM容量限制和DRAM延迟不确定性的问题。

Result: 使用26个真实世界的实验和计算电路进行评估。结果表明，Ant-Q支持1Q和2Q随机基准测试（Randomized Benchmarking）的深电路，并将电路加载和读出上行相对于执行时间的开销从22.90%-1417.05%降低到接近零。

Conclusion: Ant-Q正在被集成到QubiC 3.0中，部分功能已可用，表明该方案已在实际量子控制系统中有落地应用。

Abstract: As quantum computing continues to demonstrate promise and attract growing attention, there is an increasing need for more precise experiments to advance the development of quantum devices, as well as higher circuit throughput to validate more domain applications. However, this need is hindered by a memory bottleneck at the quantum control system layer, arising from limited on-chip BRAM capacity and the non-deterministic latency of DRAM. To break this bottleneck, we present Ant-Q, a memory hierarchy design that integrates DRAM with BRAM to support pipelined quantum circuit execution while ensuring deterministic inter-circuit timing. We evaluated Ant-Q using 26 real-world experimental and computing circuits. The results show that Ant-Q supports deep circuits for 1Q and 2Q Randomized Benchmarking and reduces the overhead of circuit loading and readout uplink relative to execution time from 22.90%-1417.05% to near zero. Ant-Q is being integrated into QubiC 3.0, with part of its functionalities already available.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [MS-MLB: An Open Machine Learning Benchmark for Blood-Based MS Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05196)
*Adam Simson,Ankush Dutta,Quang Bui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MS-MLB是一个基于全血RNA表达数据的多发性硬化症（MS）与健康对照分类的可重复开放基准。它使用公开的GSE17048数据集，在统一且防数据泄漏的评估流程下比较多种机器学习算法，包含嵌套交叉验证、独立保持集、Bootstrap置信区间、ROC/PR分析、校准度量及探索性MS研究评分。在最终基准摘要中，Gradient Boosting在保持集上以MS研究评分93.83、AUC-ROC 0.989等指标排名第一。该基准是首个聚焦于GSE17048全血RNA数据MS分类并内置外部模型提交通道的开放基准，仅用于研究比较，未经临床验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多发性硬化症的诊断依赖临床评估、MRI和实验室证据的综合判断，血液RNA表达数据虽含有疾病相关的免疫信号，但不能替代临床诊断。为了推动基于机器学习方法从全血RNA表达数据中进行MS分类研究，需要一个可重复、防泄漏的公平评估基准，以解决以往研究中评估流程不统一、不可复现的问题。

Method: 利用公开的GSE17048队列，构建MS与健康对照的二分类任务；在统一的、防泄漏控制的流程下评估多个机器学习算法。评估框架包括嵌套交叉验证、独立未触碰的保持测试集、Bootstrap置信区间、ROC与PR分析、校准度量，以及一个探索性的MS研究评分。此外，框架内还内置了文档化的外部模型提交通道。

Result: 在最终基准测试的保持集上，Gradient Boosting以MS研究评分93.83排名第一，AUC-ROC为0.989，敏感性0.950，特异性0.778，F1分数0.927，Brier分数0.050。

Conclusion: MS-MLB是首个针对GSE17048全血RNA表达数据、聚焦MS与健康对照分类并内置外部模型提交通道的开放基准。该评分仅用于研究比较参考，尚未经过临床验证。

Abstract: Multiple sclerosis (MS) is diagnosed through clinical assessment, magnetic resonance imaging, laboratory evidence when appropriate, and exclusion of better explanations. Blood RNA expression data may contain disease associated immune signal, but a blood RNA classifier cannot be treated as a replacement for clinical diagnosis. This paper presents MS-MLB (Multiple Sclerosis Machine Learning Benchmark), a reproducible open benchmark for machine learning based MS research classification from whole blood RNA expression data. MS-MLB uses the public GSE17048 cohort, converts it into an MS versus healthy control task, and evaluates multiple algorithms under a shared, leakage controlled pipeline that a researcher can rerun without reconfiguring the evaluation. The evaluation includes nested cross-validation, an untouched stratified holdout set, bootstrap confidence intervals, ROC and precision recall analysis, calibration measurement, and an exploratory MS Research Score. In the final benchmark summary, Gradient Boosting ranked first by MS Research Score on the holdout set, with an MS Research Score of 93.83, AUC-ROC of 0.989, sensitivity of 0.950, specificity of 0.778, $F_{1}$ score of 0.927, and Brier score of 0.050. Prior studies have applied machine learning to MS blood transcriptomic data, including PBMC stage classification and whole blood diagnostic signature modeling. The contribution here is different and narrower. To our knowledge, MS-MLB is the first open benchmark focused on MS versus healthy control classification from GSE17048 whole blood RNA expression data with a documented external model submission pathway built into the framework. The score is intended for research comparison only and has not been clinically validated. The benchmark is accessible here: https://github.com/duckyquang/MS-MLB.

</details>


### [117] [When Do Corrective Features Help? An Agent for Corrective Feature Discovery on Black-Box Forecasters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05207)
*Fangxin Wang,Ziyi Zhang,Diyi Zhuang,Langzhou He,Shiyu Wang,Baichuan Mo,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CRAFTER框架，通过挖掘冻结预训练预测器的残差中的可解释特征来驱动轻量级后置修正器，在不微调主干网络的情况下显著提升预测性能，在六个数据集和六个冻结主干上超越所有专用特征工程系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结的预训练预测器经常以结构化、重复性的方式失败，通过微调修复代价高昂。需要一种替代方案，能够以轻量级、可解释的方式修正冻结模型的系统性错误。

Method: CRAFTER框架包含两大候选特征生成器：(1) 对原始输入通道进行组合搜索；(2) 利用大语言模型(LLM)提出命名特征组合、二元标志和短可执行代码。两者生成的候选特征通过统一的验证集接地门控机制接受或拒绝，最后由验证集选择的正误器应用被接受的特征或保持预测不变。这种源无关的流程还允许先前特征工程系统在相同条件下进行评估。

Result: 在六个公共数据集和六个冻结主干上，CRAFTER在每个特征预算下都超越了所有专用特征工程系统，比单独使用修正器时的改进约翻倍，并将最弱主干的误差降低高达27%。这些增益在不同LLM后端上保持稳健，甚至叠加在微调主干之上仍然有效。

Conclusion: 纠正性特征发现（建模模型失败过程而非数据生成过程）是一种有效且轻量级的范式；源无关的特征生成与统一门控流程不仅带来性能提升，还使得CRAFTER成为评估不同特征工程系统贡献的通用工具。

Abstract: Frozen pretrained forecasters often fail in structured, recurring ways that are costly to repair through fine-tuning. We study corrective feature discovery: mining interpretable features of a frozen forecaster's residual to drive a lightweight post-hoc corrector. Prior automated feature engineering models the data-generating process; corrective features instead model the model-failure process. We present CRAFTER (Corrective Residual Agent with Feature-based Temporal Exploration and Reasoning), which keeps the backbone frozen and mines its residual with two complementary generators: a compositional search over the raw input channels, and a large language model (LLM) that proposes named feature combinations, binary flags, and short executable code. A single validation-grounded gate accepts or rejects every candidate regardless of its origin, and a validation-selected corrector applies the accepted features or leaves the forecast unchanged. This source-agnostic pipeline also allows prior feature-engineering systems to be evaluated under identical conditions, making CRAFTER an instrument for attributing forecast improvements to the feature source alone. Across six public datasets and six frozen backbones, CRAFTER surpasses every dedicated feature-engineering system at every feature budget, roughly doubling the improvement achieved by the corrector alone and reducing the error of the weakest backbones by up to 27%. These gains are robust across different LLM backends and persist even when applied on top of fine-tuned backbones.

</details>


### [118] [PPDL: LLM-Based Flows as Probabilistic Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05234)
*Louis Mandel,Guillaume Baudart,Mandana Vaziri,Martin Hirzel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于编写LLM驱动流程的概率编程语言，使开发者能够在整个应用流程中量化和传播不确定性，并通过实验研究和Rocq定理证明器代理的案例研究验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM虽然能力强大，但其输出往往缺乏准确性和置信度度量。在多个LLM调用和工具组成的流程中，这种不确定性会累积放大，使得开发者和终端用户难以信任最终结果。现有方法缺乏一种系统性的方式来量化和传播这种不确定性。

Method: 提出一种概率编程语言（probabilistic language），用于编写基于LLM的流程。该语言允许开发者量化不确定性并在整个应用流程中传播不确定度，同时支持在不修改流程逻辑代码的前提下试验不同的推理缩放（inference scaling）技术。

Result: 通过一个实验研究证明了该能力，并构建了一个针对Rocq定理证明器的定理证明代理作为案例研究，展示了该方法的实用性和有效性。

Conclusion: 该概率编程语言为构建可信赖的LLM应用提供了系统化的不确定性管理方案，弥合了LLM能力与可靠性之间的鸿沟，并支持灵活探索推理缩放策略。

Abstract: Building reliable applications that leverage large language models (LLMs) remains a significant challenge. While LLMs offer impressive capabilities across diverse tasks, their outputs often lack accuracy and provide no clear measure of confidence. This uncertainty compounds in flows of multiple calls to LLMs and other tools, making it difficult for developers and end-users to trust the results. This paper introduces a probabilistic language for programming LLM-based flows. It enables developers to quantify and propagate uncertainty throughout the application's flow, and experiment with different inference scaling techniques without adding a single line of code beyond the flow's logic. We present an experimental study to demonstrate this capability, and a case study building a theorem proving agent for the Rocq theorem prover.

</details>


### [119] [Decoupling Perception from Description: Computation-Grounded Representation Alignment between Multivariate Time Series and Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05238)
*Xinran Feng,Yi Xie,Chao Zhang,Ruikun Li,Wanyun Ling,Ziyue Li,Chenxi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CGTime——一个4B参数的计算接地时序-语言模型。作者通过将感知（确定性代码计算多变量时间序列的统计量）与描述（LLM对这些预计算事实进行语言化）解耦，解决了多模态时间序列-语言对齐中的"自监督陷阱"和"单变量局限"问题。CGTime在多元时间序列理解任务上超越GPT-4o-mini和GPT-5.4-nano等更大的通用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练多模态模型对齐时间序列与语言的方法存在两个核心问题：(1) 自监督陷阱——让LLM读序列写描述，标签质量受限于模型自身的感知能力，数据永远无法教给模型超出标注者已知的知识；(2) 数据集多为单变量，但真正重要的模式（跨通道相关、领先-滞后结构、共现异常）只出现在多变量场景中。这两个问题构成三难困境：现有方法无法同时满足可靠性、真实性和可扩展性。

Method: 将感知与描述解耦：确定性代码从真实开源多变量序列中计算统计量集合，LLM仅负责将这些预计算事实进行语言化表述。感知（LLM不擅长的部分）由计算完成，LLM专注文本表达。由此构建4B参数的CGTime模型。

Result: CGTime在多元理解任务上超越更大规模的通用模型：在保留基准上取得最佳多元事实分数（0.283 vs GPT-4o-mini的0.173和GPT-5.4-nano的0.203），该差距通过了针对所有基线的Holm校正配对显著性检验。此外，生成的标题中陈述可验证数值事实更准确，覆盖的统计属性范围更广。

Conclusion: 通过将感知与描述解耦，计算接地方法可以有效突破时间序列-语言对齐中的自监督天花板，在保持可靠性和真实性的同时实现可扩展性，为多变量时序理解提供了新范式。

Abstract: Training multimodal models to align time series with language runs into a self-supervision trap. The usual recipe asks an LLM to read a series and write a description, so label quality is capped by the perceptual skill the model is supposed to learn. The data can never teach more than the labeler already knows. A second gap makes this worse: most datasets use a single variable, but the patterns that matter (cross-channel correlation, lead-lag structure, co-occurring anomalies) appear only with several variables, right where the labeling LLM's limits are most exposed. These two problems create a trilemma: existing methods are reliable, realistic, or scalable, but none achieves all three. We resolve this by decoupling perception from description. Deterministic code computes a set of statistics from real, open-source multivariate series; the LLM verbalizes those precomputed facts. Perception, which LLMs do poorly, is handled by computation, while the LLM handles expression. This produces CGTime, our 4B-parameter computation-grounded time-series-language model. CGTime outperforms far larger general-purpose models on multivariate understanding tasks: it attains the best multivariate fact score on our held-out benchmark (0.283 vs. 0.173 for GPT-4o-mini and 0.203 for GPT-5.4-nano), a gap that survives Holm-corrected paired significance tests against every baseline. It also states verifiable numerical facts in generated captions more accurately and covers a broader range of statistical properties.

</details>


### [120] [Disentangling 3D Modeling from Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05242)
*Haoze Sun,Jiequan Cui,Qingshan Xu,Richang Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DiSR框架，将3D感知与空间推理显式解耦：使用现成的专家感知模型将物理世界重建为结构化3D证据，再用LoRA微调的LLM仅基于这些显式几何证据进行推理，无需大规模3D VQA训练或复杂的工具使用策略，即可在主流空间推理基准上取得有竞争力的性能，同时具备更好的可解释性、模块化和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者观察到：现代感知模型擅长估计连续3D几何信息，而LLM擅长组合式和符号式推理。这两种能力是互补的，但现有范式通常通过大规模端到端训练将两者隐式地捆绑在一起。作者希望探索一种替代范式，将3D感知与推理显式解耦，以发挥各自优势。

Method: 提出DiSR（Disentangled Spatial Reasoner）框架，包含两个阶段：(1) 使用现成的专家感知模型将物理世界重建为结构化3D证据；(2) 使用LoRA对LLM进行微调，使其仅基于这些显式几何证据进行空间推理。整个框架无需大规模3D VQA训练，也无需复杂的工具使用策略。

Result: 在主流空间推理基准上取得具有竞争力的性能，与端到端方法相比，DiSR还展现出更强的可解释性、模块化和计算效率。

Conclusion: 作者证明将感知与推理显式分离是空间智能的一种可扩展且有效的替代范式，为未来空间推理研究提供了新的思路。

Abstract: In this work, we explore an alternative paradigm for spatial reasoning by explicitly disentangling 3D perception from reasoning, rather than jointly acquiring implicit 3D perception and reasoning through large-scale training. Our key observation is that modern perception models excel at estimating continuous 3D geometry, whereas large language models (LLMs) are particularly effective at compositional and symbolic reasoning. Motivated by these complementary strengths, we propose the Disentangled Spatial Reasoner (DiSR), a simple yet effective framework that reconstructs the physical world into structured 3D evidence using off-the-shelf expert perception models and fine-tunes an LLM with LoRA to perform reasoning solely over this explicit geometric evidence. Without large-scale 3D VQA training or complex tool-use policies, DiSR achieves competitive performance on popular spatial reasoning benchmarks. Beyond its strong performance, DiSR offers improved interpretability, modularity, and computational efficiency, demonstrating that explicit separation of perception and reasoning is a scalable and effective alternative paradigm to end-to-end modeling for spatial intelligence.

</details>


### [121] [Marginal Matching Does Not License Factorized Sampling: Auditing Conditional Style Leakage in Factorized Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05243)
*Duong Bach,Hai Nguyen Hong,Cuong Do*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 因子化生成模型通常通过匹配潜在风格变量的边缘分布到高斯先验来声称风格表示与类别信息无关。本文证明这种解释是错误的：边缘匹配无法约束类别条件分布，风格潜伏变量仍可高度预测标签。作者推导出精确分解，表明消除这一不匹配是实现因子化采样的四个必要条件之一，但并非充分条件。实验表明多种基线在全局MMD接近零的情况下，线性探针仍能以74%-100%的准确率恢复类别标签（随机水平10%）。多种缓解策略只能部分降低泄漏，且任何仅基于风格潜变量边缘分布的散度度量都无法证明与类别标签的独立性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示因子化生成模型中一个普遍的误解：将风格潜变量边缘分布匹配到固定高斯先验被错误地当作风格表示与类别信息独立的证据。作者旨在证明这种解读在数学上不成立，并系统性地阐明边缘匹配与真正的因子化解纠缠之间的差距。

Method: 作者首先推导出一个精确分解，将因子化采样所需的四个条件与边缘分布匹配问题联系起来，证明消除边缘不匹配是必要但不充分条件。随后设计了一个案例研究模型和四种代表性潜在基线，在MNIST和CIFAR-10上进行系统实验，使用全局MMD、线性探针、聚类准确性、外部类别条件生成等多种评估方法，并测试了六种扰动（模型容量、课程学习、先验几何、监督程度等）和四种缓解策略及两种后处理方案。

Result: 所有基线的全局MMD接近零，但线性探针仍能以74%-100%的准确率恢复类别标签（随机水平10%）。案例模型聚类准确率达99.15%，而外部类别条件生成仅成功16%。四种缓解策略将探针准确率降至21%-46%，但未显著改变类内依赖。事后条件先验在MNIST上实现0.97的类别生成分数，在CIFAR-10上仅达0.41，而经验风格库在CIFAR-10上达到0.88。

Conclusion: 仅基于风格潜变量边缘分布的散度度量（如MMD）无法证明其与类别标签的独立性。报告边缘统计量不足以验证因子化生成模型所声称的独立性属性。研究量化了边缘匹配与实际因子化解纠缠之间的差距，并提出了更具验证力的评估方向，强调需要使用类别条件分布或依赖关系的度量来替代单纯报告边缘统计量。

Abstract: Factorized generative models commonly regularize a latent style variable z_s by matching its marginal distribution to a fixed Gaussian prior and interpret this as evidence that the style representation is independent of class information. We show that this interpretation is incorrect. Matching only the marginal distribution places no constraint on the class-conditional distributions, allowing the latent style to remain highly predictive of the label despite appearing perfectly Gaussian in aggregate. We derive an exact decomposition showing that this mismatch is one of four conditions required for factorized sampling, and demonstrate that eliminating it is necessary but not sufficient to obtain the intended factorization.
  Empirically, our case-study model and four representative latent baselines achieve near-zero global MMD while still allowing a linear probe to recover class labels with 74%--100% accuracy (10% chance level). Our model reaches 99.15% clustering accuracy, whereas externally evaluated class-conditional generation succeeds only 16% of the time. This leakage remains under six independent perturbations involving model capacity, curriculum, prior geometry, and supervision across two datasets. Four mitigation strategies reduce probe accuracy to 21%--46%, although they leave within-class dependence largely unchanged. A post-hoc conditional prior improves externally evaluated class generation to 0.97 on MNIST without retraining but reaches only 0.41 on CIFAR-10, while an empirical style bank achieves 0.88 on CIFAR-10. These results demonstrate that no divergence computed solely on the marginal distribution of the style latent can certify independence from class labels, and that reporting marginal statistics alone does not verify the property commonly claimed in factorized generative models.

</details>


### [122] [PRISM: Priority-aware Rubric Internalization via Structured Multimodal Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05249)
*Xiaomin He,Dongling Xiao,Jiahao Xie,Ruiqi Lu,Qianle Wang,Zhongbin Guo,Wanxuan Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PRISM框架，通过四阶段数据合成流程生成带优先级的多规则语言模型训练数据，并引入PRISM-Eval评测基准（含Loose和Strict指标），使模型从"生成者"转变为"规则执行者"。仅用10K合成样本，PRISM将Qwen3-VL-4B的Strict准确率从9.5%提升至30.1%，且增益可迁移至多个开源MLLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的多模态指令通常包含多条重要性不同的要求，但现有大多数多模态训练数据仍将指令遵循简化为回答单一自包含问题，缺少对多条规则及优先级感知的建模。作者希望通过"rubric comprehension"（规则理解）这一新范式弥补这一鸿沟。

Method: PRISM是一个四阶段数据合成框架：（1）生成persona-task配对；（2）产生前缀引导的规则集；（3）生成经过质量过滤的规则清单（rubrics）；（4）生成结构化验证轨迹。同时提出PRISM-Eval评测基准，其Loose和Strict指标基于确定性匹配固定标签，无需推理时的评判模型。

Result: 仅使用10K合成样本，PRISM将Qwen3-VL-4B在PRISM-Eval上的Strict准确率从9.5%提升至30.1%，同时保持通用基准上的平均性能。增益可迁移至另外四个开源多模态大语言模型，涵盖dense和MoE架构。

Conclusion: 结构化规则监督是通向多规则、优先级感知的多模态指令遵循的可扩展路径，PRISM框架及其评测工具为该方向提供了数据生成与评估的完整方案。

Abstract: Real-world multimodal instructions often bundle multiple requirements with unequal importance, yet most multimodal training data still reduce instruction following to answering one self-contained question. We study this gap through \textbf{rubric comprehension}, which casts the model not as a generator measured against rubrics but as an \textbf{executor} that follows them: given an image and a typed, prioritized rubric, the model must verify each rule before producing an overall judgment. To support this setting, we propose \textbf{PRISM}, a four-stage data synthesis framework that produces persona--task pairs, prefix-guided rule sets, quality-filtered rubrics, and structured verification traces. We further introduce \textbf{PRISM-Eval}, whose Loose and Strict metrics use deterministic matching against fixed labels and therefore require no inference-time judge model. With only 10K synthesized samples, PRISM lifts Qwen3-VL-4B from 9.5\% to 30.1\% Strict accuracy on PRISM-Eval while preserving average performance on general benchmarks, and the gains transfer to four additional open-source MLLMs across dense and MoE architectures, suggesting that structured rubric supervision is a scalable path toward multi-rule, priority-aware multimodal instruction following.

</details>


### [123] [Beyond Full-Model Rollback: AuroSFT for Adapter-State Multi-Task Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05250)
*Yue Han,Ziniu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AuroSFT，一种参数高效的过拟合感知多任务监督微调框架，将msft的全模型检查点状态重新设计为紧凑、可合并的适配器状态，冻结预训练骨干网络，仅训练注入的适配器，在任务级峰值回滚适配器检查点。在保留骨干对比协议下，AuroSFT在五个骨干网络上均取得更高平均准确率（61.36% vs 59.85%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务SFT通常将异构数据混合视为单一优化问题，但不同任务可能在不同时间达到最佳泛化。msft通过任务级回滚、排除等机制解决了这一问题，但其原始实现将调度器状态物化为完整模型检查点，导致阶段转换在存储、恢复和部署上成本高昂。

Method: AuroSFT冻结预训练骨干网络，仅训练注入的适配器，在任务级峰值处回滚适配器检查点，并继续在剩余活跃混合数据上训练。在层级别，每个适配器应用AuroRA启发的自适应非线性层到低秩权重因子（而非样本表示），所得更新对输入保持线性、秩有界，且可精确合并到冻结投影中。

Result: 在保留骨干对比协议下，AuroSFT平均准确率达61.36%，相比对应的msft参考行59.85%提升了1.51个百分点；在所有五个骨干网络上均取得更高准确率。

Conclusion: AuroSFT通过参数高效的可合并适配器状态，解决了msft中全模型检查点的存储与部署开销问题，同时提升了多任务SFT的过拟合感知调度性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multi-task supervised fine-tuning (SFT) often casts a heterogeneous data mixture as a single optimization problem, even though different tasks may reach their best generalization at different times. msft exposes this mismatch through task-wise roll-out, exclusion, and rollback, but its original formulation materializes the scheduler state as full-model checkpoints, making stage transitions costly to store, restore, and deploy. This paper introduces AuroSFT, a parameter-efficient framework that recasts the carried state of overfitting-aware multi-task SFT as a compact, mergeable adapter state. AuroSFT freezes the pretrained backbone, trains only injected adapters, rolls back adapter checkpoints at task-wise peaks, and continues on the remaining active mixture. At the layer level, each adapter applies an AuroRA-inspired adaptive nonlinear layer to a low-rank weight factor rather than to the sample representation. The resulting update remains linear in the input, rank-bounded, and exactly mergeable into the frozen projection. Under the retained-backbone comparison protocol, AuroSFT achieves 61.36% average accuracy, compared with 59.85% for the corresponding msft reference row, and obtains higher accuracy on all five backbones. Our code is available at the anonymous repository: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/AuroSFT-80D1.

</details>


### [124] [Beyond Rotations: AuroOFT for Expressive Quantized Orthogonal Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05253)
*Yue Han,Dianlin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AuroOFT是一种增强的量化正交微调方法，通过在QOFT的线性正交变换分支旁附加零初始化的门控低秩非线性残差，实现低比特语言模型的参数高效适应，在多个模型规模上优于QOFT和QLoRA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QOFT虽然实现了低比特语言模型的参数高效微调，但其任务特定更新被限制在线性正交变换范围内，无法进行依赖于输入的非线性校正，这限制了微调的表达能力。

Method: AuroOFT在保持QOFT作为稳定的量化兼容分支的同时，为每个适配的线性层附加零初始化门控低秩非线性残差。它将激活映射到RMS归一化的紧凑潜在空间，并使用带有有界或令牌相关门控的自适应非线性基。零初始化的上投影使AuroOFT在初始化时与QOFT功能等价，正交性仅作为分支级稳定属性而非组合非线性层的属性。

Result: 在匹配的数据、优化、解码和解析协议下，AuroOFT在1.5B/3B Qwen2.5设置上相比匹配的QOFT将Macro-6提高了1.30-2.70%，超过QLoRA 6.52-10.62%，并在代表性规模下比QLoRA节省了32.3-44.7%的可训练参数。

Conclusion: 通过添加输入相关的非线性校正分支，AuroOFT突破了QOFT线性正交变换的表达限制，在保持量化兼容稳定性的同时获得了显著的性能提升和参数效率优势。

Abstract: Quantized orthogonal fine-tuning (qoft) enables parameter-efficient adaptation of low-bit language models by learning structured activation rotations before frozen quantized weights. However, its task-specific updates remain constrained to linear orthogonal transformations, limiting input-dependent nonlinear corrections. We introduce AuroOFT, which keeps qoft as a stable quantization-compatible branch while attaching a zero-start gated low-rank nonlinear residual to each adapted linear layer. AuroOFT maps activations into an RMS-normalized compact latent space and uses adaptive nonlinear bases with bounded or token-dependent gating. The zero-initialized up projection makes AuroOFT functionally identical to qoft at initialization, while orthogonality remains a branch-level stability property rather than a property of the combined nonlinear layer. Under matched data, optimization, decoding, and parser protocols, AuroOFT improves Macro-6 over matched qoft by 1.30-2.70% on the 1.5B/3B Qwen2.5 settings, exceeds QLoRA by 6.52-10.62%, and saves 32.3-44.7% trainable parameters relative to QLoRA in representative scales. The small exam-style multiple-choice math set is treated only as a protocol-sensitivity diagnostic. Our code is available at the anonymous repository: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/AuroOFT-F3FD.

</details>


### [125] [An Emerging Retail Portfolio Management Application: Personalized, Tax-Aware Reinforcement Learning with Natural Language Goals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05255)
*Ramin Pishehvar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个面向个人投资者的智能化、税负感知的机器人投顾系统。系统包含FastAPI后端和Web仪表盘，通过自然语言描述投资目标，路由到六种投资任务之一，并由一个三阶段强化学习系统（自监督跨资产编码器、MoE配置策略、LoRA个性化适配器）生成实时、接入券商API的投资组合推荐。系统已端到端集成测试（Alpaca纸面交易模式），但尚未开放给真实用户，目前处于部署前阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人投资者无法获得机构客户享有的个性化、税负感知的投资组合管理服务。现有机器人投顾采用静态、基于规则的资产配置，而机构级系统又需要最低账户金额和机构级技术栈，普通投资者无法使用。

Method: 采用三阶段强化学习系统：(1) 自监督跨资产编码器；(2) 基于Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）的配置策略，配有一个学习的意图路由器，将自然语言目标路由到六个投资任务之一；(3) LoRA适配器，利用用户已披露的经纪行为个性化推荐，无需重训共享模型。系统还包含多用户认证、预览后确认的信任流程、每日邮件摘要和可审计的行动完整性链。

Result: 系统功能完整并通过端到端集成测试（针对Alpaca纸面交易API），包括多用户认证、信任优先的预览-确认流程、每日邮件摘要和可审计操作链。提供了14天滚动前向回测（含自助法置信区间）作为部署前初步验证。还报告了若干工程经验，如静默失效的集成路径、第三方API调用挂起、以及端到端实证验证优于信任检查点元数据。

Conclusion: 这是一个诚实地自我报告为"部署前新兴应用"的系统，具有明确的全部署路径。尽管尚未上线给真实用户，系统已通过实际券商API完成端到端集成验证，并提供了可泛化到其他构建在实时外部数据源上的强化学习系统的工程经验。

Abstract: Retail investors lack access to the kind of personalized, tax-aware portfolio management that institutional clients take for granted -- existing robo-advisors use static, rule-based allocation, and institutional-grade systems require account minimums and technology stacks unavailable to individual investors. We present a fully built, integration-tested application that closes this gap: a FastAPI backend and web dashboard that let a user describe an investment goal in plain language (e.g. "I want steady growth but need to sell some shares next month for a down payment"), routes that goal to one of six investment mandates, and produces a live, broker-integrated portfolio recommendation from athree-phase reinforcement learning system -- a self-supervised cross-asset encoder, a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) allocation policy with a learned intent router, and a lightweight LoRA adapter that personalizes recommendations from an individual's revealed brokerage behavior without retraining the shared model. The system is functionally complete and integration-tested end-to-end against a live brokerage API (Alpaca, paper-trading mode), including multi-user authentication, a trust first preview-before-apply confirmation flow, daily email digests, and an auditable action-integrity chain, but has not yet been opened to real end-users; we report this honestly as an emerging, pre-deployment application with a concrete path to full deployment, alongside 14-day walk-forward backtests (bootstrapped confidence intervals included) as preliminary, pre-deployment validation rather than production performance. We also report several practical engineering lessons -- silently-inactive integration paths, hanging third-party API calls, and the value of end-to-end empirical verification over trusting checkpoint metadata -- that we believe generalize to other applied RL systems built on external, live data sources.

</details>


### [126] [Evaluating Machine Learning Models for Post-Wildfire Debris-Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05265)
*Quinn Ledingham,Zhengsen Xu,Yimin Zhu,Zack Dewis,Mabel Heffring,Saeid Taleghanidoozdoozan,Motasem Alkayid,Megan Greenwood,Lincoln Linlin Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了15种机器学习模型（包括TabPFN）用于野火后泥石流预测，通过重复分层交叉验证、SHAP特征重要性分析和合成数据增强，发现TabPFN在无增强数据下性能最优（威胁评分0.637），短时降雨强度和风暴累积量是最重要的预测特征，合成数据增强可提升除CNN外所有模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野火后泥石流预测对减轻灾害至关重要，但现有挑战包括：泥石流与非泥石流事件在特征空间中重叠导致分类困难、模型可解释性需求、以及训练数据有限的困境。作者旨在通过系统化评估多种机器学习模型，结合特征重要性和合成数据增强来解决这些问题。

Method: 使用美国西部流域尺度的野火后泥石流观测数据，对15种机器学习模型（包括TabPFN和多种树模型）进行系统比较。采用重复分层交叉验证评估预测性能，使用SHAP分析特征重要性，并利用TabPFN生成合成样本进行数据增强以缓解训练数据稀缺问题。

Result: TabPFN在无增强数据下取得最高威胁评分0.637，其次是表现最好的树模型。SHAP分析表明短时降雨强度和风暴累积量是预测的关键特征，而燃烧严重度和地形特征贡献较小。合成数据增强使除CNN外所有模型的性能均有所提升，深度学习模型平均威胁评分提升最大，达+0.041。

Conclusion: 该工作通过严格的模型基准测试、可解释的特征分析和合成数据增强，为改进野火后泥石流预测提供了一个综合框架，证明了TabPFN在该任务中的优越性，并验证了合成数据增强作为缓解数据稀缺问题的有效策略。

Abstract: Prediction of post-wildfire debris flows is critical for mitigating hazards to communities, infrastructure, and resources during intense rainfall in recently burned areas. However, identifying reliable machine learning models is complicated by overlapping debris-flow and non-debris-flow events in feature space, the need for model interpretability, and limited training data. This paper addresses these challenges through a systematic evaluation of machine learning models in terms of predictive performance, feature importance, and synthetic data augmentation. Using basin-scale observations of post-wildfire debris-flow events across the western United States, we compare 15 models, including the Tabular Prior-Data Fitted Network (TabPFN). Repeated stratified cross-validation shows that TabPFN achieves the highest unaugmented performance with a threat score of 0.637, closely followed by the best tree-based models. SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) are used to identify the features driving predictions, revealing that short-duration rainfall intensity and storm accumulation consistently rank highest, while burn severity and terrain features contribute less. We further evaluate synthetic data augmentation using TabPFN-generated samples to address the scarcity of debris-flow observations. Synthetic augmentation improves the performance of all models except CNN, with the largest mean threat score increase of +0.041 among the deep learning models. By combining rigorous model benchmarking, interpretable feature analysis, and synthetic data augmentation, this work provides a comprehensive framework for improving post-wildfire debris-flow prediction.

</details>


### [127] [Rectifying Geometric Misalignment: Online Source-Free Adaptation for Class-Imbalanced EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05315)
*Shiwen Chu,Shanglin Li,Motoaki Kawanabe,Reinmar Kobler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OSPDIM，一种源无监督在线域自适应框架，通过在黎曼流形的切空间映射中引入流形约束的偏置参数并经信息最大化优化，来解决EEG-BCI在线场景中因类别分布不平衡（标签偏移）导致的标准黎曼对齐方法几何错位问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实在线BCI场景中标签分布动态变化，导致标签偏移，而现有黎曼对齐方法（如RCT）隐含假设类别先验平衡，面对不平衡数据流时会产生几何错位，导致对齐失效。

Method: 提出OSPDIM框架：在切空间映射中引入受流形约束的偏置参数，通过信息最大化优化该参数以在线纠正不平衡数据流导致的几何歪斜；与依赖全局批次统计的离线方法不同，OSPDIM实现动态在线估计与校正。

Result: 在2D SPD矩阵仿真中可视化验证了OSPDIM能纠正标准中心化无法解决的错位问题；在多个运动想象数据集上的大量实验表明，OSPDIM在严重类别不平衡的在线自适应场景下显著优于标准黎曼基线方法。

Conclusion: OSPDIM为实际即插即用的BCI系统提供了应对在线类别不平衡场景的鲁棒解决方案，有效弥补了传统黎曼对齐方法在标签偏移情况下的不足。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) based Brain-Computer Interfaces (BCIs) often require unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA) to generalize across subjects and sessions. While Riemannian alignment methods like the Riemannian Centering Transformation (RCT) are effective for handling covariate shifts, they implicitly assume balanced class priors. However, in realistic online BCI scenarios, the label distributions vary dynamically (label shift), causing standard alignment techniques to geometrically misalign the target data distributions. In this work, we propose OSPDIM (Online SPD manifold information maximization), a source-free online UDA framework designed to address label shifts on the Riemannian manifold. OSPDIM introduces a manifold-constrained bias parameter into the tangent space mapping, which is optimized via information maximization to correct the geometric skew caused by imbalanced data streams. Unlike offline methods relying on global batch statistics, OSPDIM estimates and corrects geometric bias on-the-fly. Simulations on 2D SPD matrices visually demonstrate that OSPDIM successfully rectifies the misalignment where standard centering fails. Extensive experiments on multiple motor imagery datasets show that OSPDIM significantly outperforms standard Riemannian baselines, particularly in challenging online adaptation scenarios with severe class imbalance, offering a robust solution for practical, plug-and-play BCI systems.

</details>


### [128] [QEvict: Recoverable Quantized KV Eviction for Attention-Drift-Robust Long-Context Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05326)
*Ayushman Garg,Akshita Gupta,Shaswata Bhattacharya,Abhishek Gupta,Sandeep Kumar,Manoj Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QEvict，一种三层KV缓存管理方案，用可恢复的逐出机制取代传统的二值保留-删除策略，解决LLM推理中KV缓存压缩导致的重要token被永久误逐出的问题。QEvict将高置信度窗口保留全精度、中间窗口存入量化可恢复层、仅删除最低置信度窗口，并在解码过程中动态地将重新变得重要的量化窗口提升回全精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归LLM推理受限于KV缓存的内存占用，主流方法通过注意力分数逐出"不重要"的token来降低内存。然而这种逐出策略是隐式的不可逆决策——token一旦被逐出就无法重新变得有用。作者发现这一假设在解码过程中是脆弱的：token和窗口的重要性会随着生成查询的演化而漂移，导致标准逐出策略永久丢弃那些在完整缓存模型下后来会获得大量注意力的状态。

Method: 1) 提出两个诊断指标：Future Missed Mass（衡量被丢弃状态未来获得的注意力总量）和Global LIR（衡量历史非活跃区域的重新激活程度），用于刻画token重要性漂移现象。2) 提出QEvict三层KV缓存管理系统：第一层将高置信度窗口以全精度保留；第二层将中间窗口存储在量化可恢复层；第三层仅删除最低置信度窗口。3) 解码过程中，累积注意力分数持续更新窗口重要性，当量化窗口重新变得重要时，将其去量化并提升到全精度层。

Result: 在长上下文理解、检索和推理基准上，QEvict在固定内存预算下始终优于代表性逐出方法和量化基线，减少了被遗漏的注意力并改善了信息保留。相比二值逐出策略，QEvict在保留更广泛历史上下文的同时，对最重要区域保持精确的全精度。

Conclusion: QEvict证明可恢复的逐出机制优于不可逆的二值删除策略：通过引入量化中间层实现token状态的"软删除"，允许历史状态在需要时重新激活，从而更契合解码过程中token重要性动态漂移的实际分布。这一设计为KV缓存压缩提供了一条新的技术路线。

Abstract: Autoregressive large language model inference is increasingly constrained by the memory footprint of the Key-Value (KV) cache. A dominant line of work reduces this footprint by evicting tokens that appear unimportant under attention-derived scores. However, such policies make an implicit irreversible decision: once a token is evicted, it cannot become useful again. We show that this assumption is brittle during decoding. Token and window importance drift as generated queries evolve, causing standard eviction policies to permanently discard states that later receive substantial attention under the full-cache model. To characterize this behaviour, we introduce Future Missed Mass and Global LIR, two diagnostics that measure future attention assigned to discarded states and the reactivation of historically inactive regions. We propose QEvict, a three-tier KV-cache management scheme that replaces binary retain-or-delete eviction with recoverable eviction. QEvict maintains high-confidence windows in full precision, stores intermediate windows in a quantized recoverable tier, and deletes only the lowest-confidence windows. During decoding, cumulative attention scores update window importance and when a quantized window becomes important again, it is dequantized and promoted to the full-precision. Under a fixed memory budget, this design preserves broader historical context while retaining exact full precision for the most important regions. Across long-context understanding, retrieval, and reasoning benchmarks, QEvict consistently improves over representative eviction and quantization baselines, reducing missed attention and improving information retention

</details>


### [129] [DG-FedReuse: Proxy-Gradient-Gated Cached-Update Reuse with Matched Sparse Uplink Accounting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05358)
*Rahil Aftab,Vineet Kumar Rakesh,Soumya Mazumdar,Tapas Samanta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DG-FedReuse，一种模拟器级别的联邦学习机制，允许选定的客户端在随机头梯度差异代理低于轮次相关阈值时贡献按年龄衰减的缓存更新。在90轮预算下，该机制可实现83.36-85.42%的上行更新数据字段节省（对比Top-K FedAvg的76.88%），但考虑对称的稠密模型下行传输后，优势显著缩小至41.68-42.71%。作者强调该研究仅为模拟器层面的机制表征，未声称无偏泛化、端到端带宽节省或运行时/能量优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习反复进行局部优化和模型更新传输，通信开销是核心瓶颈。现有方法已有Top-K压缩和缓存更新等思路，但缺乏一种同时结合缓存重用（按年龄衰减）与随机头梯度差异判断的模拟器级机制的精细化评估。作者希望量化这种机制的上行通信节省效果，并明确其在现实约束（如下行传输）下的边界。

Method: 提出DG-FedReuse机制：1）设定轮次相关的阈值，利用随机头梯度差异代理判断是否允许客户端使用按年龄衰减的缓存更新；2）设置硬性缓存年龄上限和最小新鲜客户端配额来约束重用；3）新鲜更新采用自适应逐张量Top-K数值字段表示。实验使用6个图像分类数据集、50个虚拟客户端、Dirichlet标签异质性（α=0.5）和3个种子，并与匹配的Top-K FedAvg对照。

Result: 在90轮预算下，DG-FedReuse实现83.36-85.42%的模型更新数据字段上行节省（Top-K FedAvg为76.88%），种子对齐的准确率差异为-5.29到-0.14个百分点。测试控制检查点下的最佳观测准确率为-2.38到+0.45个百分点。考虑对称稠密模型下行传输后，总节省降至41.68-42.71%，相对于Top-K FedAvg的增量优势缩小至3.24-4.27个百分点。

Conclusion: DG-FedReuse在上行通信节省方面表现出色，但通信结论高度依赖计算边界（是否计入下行传输）。作者明确声明该研究仅描述模拟器中的重用规则，不建立无偏泛化保证，也不声称端到端带宽、运行时或能量节省的普适性，更不主张其优于现有的陈旧更新和惰性聚合方法。

Abstract: Federated learning repeatedly incurs local optimization and model-update transmission. We study DG-FedReuse, a simulator-level mechanism that allows selected clients to contribute age-decayed cached updates when a stochastic head-gradient discrepancy proxy remains below a round-dependent threshold. A hard cache-age limit and minimum fresh-client quota constrain reuse, while fresh updates use an adaptive per-tensor Top-K numerical-field representation. Experiments cover six image-classification datasets, 50 virtual clients, Dirichlet label heterogeneity (α=0.5), and three seeds. At a common 90-round budget, DG-FedReuse yields 83.36-85.42% modeled update-data-field uplink saving, compared with 76.88% for matched Top-K FedAvg; the seed-aligned accuracy differences range from -5.29 to -0.14 percentage points. Best-observed test accuracies obtained under test-controlled checkpointing are retained only as exploratory archival evidence and range from -2.38 to +0.45 percentage points relative to matched FedAvg. A symmetric dense-model-downlink sensitivity reduces the headline saving to 41.68-F42.71% and the incremental gain over Top-K FedAvg to 3.24-4.27 percentage points, demonstrating the dependence of communication conclusions on the accounting boundary. The study characterizes the proposed reuse rule in the implemented simulator; it does not establish unbiased generalization, end-to-end bandwidth reduction, runtime or energy savings, faster convergence, or superiority over existing stale-update and lazy-aggregation methods.

</details>


### [130] [Quantum-Structured World Models (QSWMs) for Predictive Latent Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05371)
*Hailong Jiang,Emran Hossain,Feng Yu,Jianfeng Zhu,Guilin Zhang,Wulan Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出量子结构化世界模型（QSWMs），一种受量子理论启发的预测世界建模框架，使用复数表示和密度矩阵式潜在状态。作者建立了三个基础性质（经典包含性、预测充分性、结构化紧凑性），并在元胞自动机上与经典基线对比，结果显示复数变体具有良好的局部预测潜力，但长时程推演和密度矩阵变体存在局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的世界模型大多使用经典向量、概率分布、循环隐藏状态或Transformer激活来表示潜在状态。作者希望探索量子理论中的数学结构（如复数表示和密度矩阵式潜在状态）是否能提供有益于世界建模的归纳偏置，从而为世界模型提供新的表征范式。

Method: 本文提出了三个基础性质：经典包含性（经典表示包含在QSWM中）、预测充分性（足以支持预测）、结构化紧凑性（紧凑的状态结构）。并在此基础上实例化了复数表示和密度矩阵式两种QSWM变体，在初等元胞自动机（elementary cellular automata）上对强经典基线进行评估。

Result: 实验结果显示复数表示QSWM在局部预测上表现出有前景的潜力，但长时程推演（long-horizon rollout）存在限制；密度矩阵变体在实验中表现不如预期。

Conclusion: 量子启发式结构在一定场景下具有预测价值，但当前方法在长时程序列推演和密度矩阵式潜在状态方面仍有明显局限，为未来量子启发世界模型的研究指明了改进方向。

Abstract: World models learn latent states that summarize interaction histories, evolve over time, and support prediction, simulation, or planning. Most existing world models represent these states using classical vectors, probability distributions, recurrent hidden states, or transformer activations. In this paper, we introduce Quantum-Structured World Models (QSWMs), a quantum-inspired framework for predictive world modeling with structured latent states, latent transition operators, and measurement-inspired decoding maps. We study whether mathematical structures inspired by quantum theory, such as complex-valued representations and density-matrix-like latents, provide useful inductive biases for world modeling. We establish three foundational properties: classical inclusion, predictive sufficiency, and structured compactness. We then instantiate complex-valued and density-matrix-like QSWM variants and evaluate them on elementary cellular automata against strong classical baselines. Results show promising local predictive potential for complex-valued QSWMs, while also revealing limitations in long-horizon rollout, density-matrix variants

</details>


### [131] [Spectral Distillation: From Nonlinear Dynamics to Linear State-Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05416)
*Liane Galanti,Devan Shah,Shlomo Fortgang,Elad Hazan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过凸优化和蒸馏方法从非线性动力系统中学习紧凑线性状态空间表示（LDS）的可证明流水线。核心思路是先使用观测谱滤波（OSF）学习隐式谱预测器，再通过谱到LDS的蒸馏将其转化为显式递归线性动力系统。理论保证显示预测误差可分解为指数小的蒸馏项和由Luenberger复杂度主导的学习项，且该保证与维度无关。实验验证该方法在LDS基准和MuJoCo行为克隆任务上优于直接训练的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性动力系统的辨识通常需要求解非凸优化问题，这是困难的。作者希望回答一个关键问题：能否通过紧凑的线性状态空间表示来学习非线性动力系统，而无需直接求解非凸的系统辨识问题？目标是设计一个可证明的端到端方法。

Method: 提出两阶段流水线：（1）观测谱滤波（OSF）：一种凸方法，用于学习隐式谱预测器，与系统的最佳线性观测器竞争；（2）谱到LDS蒸馏：将隐式预测器转化为显式的递归线性动力系统。核心定理证明蒸馏后的LDS平均预测误差由两个部分构成：指数小的蒸馏项和由最佳观测器的Luenberger复杂度决定的OSF学习项。

Result: 该方法的理论保证是维度无关的，依赖于观测器复杂度而非表示非线性系统所需的潜在维度。这是首个通过凸学习后接可证明蒸馏来提取非线性动力学最佳事后LDS表示的端到端可证明方法。实验表明，在LDS基准测试和MuJoCo行为克隆任务中，训练后蒸馏流水线生成的紧凑LDS预测器与直接训练的基线相当或更优。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了通过凸优化加蒸馏的方式可以有效且可证明地从非线性动力系统中提取紧凑线性表示，避开了非凸系统辨识难题，并在理论与实验上都取得了令人信服的结果。

Abstract: Can nonlinear dynamical systems be learned through a compact linear state-space representation, without directly solving a non-convex system-identification problem? We give a provable pipeline for doing so. Starting from observations of an unknown nonlinear dynamical system, we first learn an implicit spectral predictor using Observation Spectral Filtering (OSF), a convex method that competes with the best linear observer for the system. We then apply spectral-to-LDS distillation to convert this predictor into an explicit recurrent linear dynamical system. Our main theorem shows that the average prediction error of the distilled LDS decomposes into an exponentially-small distillation term and the OSF learning term governed by the Luenberger complexity of the best observer. The guarantee is dimension-free: it depends on observer complexity rather than on the latent dimension needed to represent the nonlinear system. To our knowledge, this yields the first end-to-end provable method for extracting a best-in-hindsight LDS representation of nonlinear dynamics through convex learning followed by provable distillation. Experiments on linear LDS benchmarks and MuJoCo behavior cloning show that the train-then-distill pipeline produces compact LDS predictors that match or outperform directly trained baselines.

</details>


### [132] [Perturbation Sensitivity at Convergence: A Simple Signal for Identifying Spuriously Correlated Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05419)
*Nilesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需群体标注的虚假相关性样本检测方法：通过对训练后的模型输入施加固定扰动，利用两次前向传播即可区分与虚假相关规则一致的样本和脆弱的不一致样本，进而重平衡训练数据，显著提升最差组准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经验风险最小化模型在含有虚假相关性的数据上取得高平均准确率，但在相关性不成立的子群体上表现失败。现有无需群体标注的检测方法依赖早期训练信号，但需要利用带组标注的验证数据选取干预纪元这一超参数，限制了实用性。

Method: 作者发现模型收敛后，损失已无法区分两类群体，但存在可用信号：与虚假相关一致的样本由共享规则分类，其余样本则由输入特定的配置拟合，因此更脆弱。方法对收敛模型的输入施加固定扰动，脆弱样本的预测翻转频率显著更高。整个过程仅需每个训练样本两次前向传播，无需任何阶段的组标注，也无需选择早期停止纪元。

Result: 在Waterbirds数据集上，利用检测出的样本重平衡训练，最差组准确率从57.3%提升至80.8%，而使用真实组标注的对照结果为85.8%。

Conclusion: 收敛后模型对固定输入扰动的预测脆弱性可作为区分虚假相关与擦除样本的有效信号，该信号不依赖早期训练动态，为无需群体标注的鲁棒性提升提供了更简洁实用的方案。

Abstract: Models trained by empirical risk minimization on data containing spurious correlations achieve high average accuracy while failing on subpopulations where the correlation does not hold. Existing methods for identifying the affected samples without group annotations rely on signals from early training, which requires locating the epoch at which to intervene, a hyperparameter typically selected using group-labeled validation data. We show that a usable signal is available after convergence, when loss no longer distinguishes the two populations. Samples consistent with the spurious correlation are classified by a shared rule, while the remaining samples are fit through configurations specific to individual inputs and are correspondingly more fragile. Applying a fixed perturbation to a converged model's inputs flips the predictions of the latter far more often than the former. The resulting procedure requires two forward passes per training sample, no group annotations at any stage, and no early-stopping epoch. Using the detected samples to rebalance training raises worst-group accuracy on Waterbirds from 57.3% to 80.8%, against 85.8% with ground-truth group labels.

</details>


### [133] [IFlowNets: Extending Generative Samplers to Learn Strategies in Incomplete Information Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05422)
*Conor M. Artman,Nicholas Di,Scott Perkins*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出信息流网络（IFNs），将生成流网络框架从完全信息博弈扩展到不完全信息博弈，证明原有约束在此场景下失效，并提出广义化方法IFlowNets，在三个标准博弈环境中性能与速度上优于或持平OSMCCFR与标准RL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然已有大量工作将强化学习（RL）与反事实遗憾（CFR）方法结合以权衡计算速度与性能，但在不完全信息博弈中，对生成式采样框架的探索相对较少。作者希望填补这一空白，将生成流网络（GFlowNets）框架推广到不完全信息博弈场景。

Method: 作者将对抗流网络（AFlowNets）框架扩展到不完全信息博弈，提出信息流网络（IFNs）。首先证明了在完全信息博弈中已建立的生成流网络约束在不完全信息博弈中不可行，无法获得有效的密度（对应玩家策略）和有效的训练目标。随后提出广义化方法IFlowNets，该框架能解决上述问题并严格推广AFlowNets。

Result: 在三个标准博弈环境的初步实验中，IFlowNets在性能和速度上与Outcome Sampling Monte Carlo Counterfactual Regret（OSMCCFR）和标准RL方法相比，表现相当或更优。

Conclusion: IFlowNets成功将生成流网络框架推广至不完全信息博弈，解决了原有约束的不可行性问题，且在实践中表现出不错的性能与计算效率，为不完全信息博弈中的生成式采样方法提供了新思路。

Abstract: While many algorithms blend reinforcement learning (RL) with counterfactual regret (CFR) methods to leverage tradeoffs in computational speed and performance, there are fewer investigations into generative sampling frameworks in game theoretic applications in incomplete information games. We extend a generative flow network framework, Adversarial Flow Networks (AFlowNets), to incomplete information games, called Information Flow Networks (IFNs). We prove that previously established constraints for generative flow networks in complete information games are inadmissible for obtaining valid densities (corresponding to player strategies) and a valid training objective. We show that our proposed generalization, IFlowNets, alleviates this issue and strictly generalizes AFlowNets. In preliminary results for three standard game environments, IFlowNets perform comparably to or better than Outcome Sampling Monte Carlo Counterfactual Regret (OSMCCFR) and standard RL-based methods in performance and speed.

</details>


### [134] [Why the Third Axis Is Freedom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05423)
*Michael Timothy Bennett*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示"探索建模"(XM)所谓"第三预训练轴"的本质是自由(freedom)——即模型行为所隐含约束的强弱。作者证明XM的平均损失取决于候选输出命中可接受区域的概率(以K次幂形式提升),并从理论和实验两方面论证XM优化的正是自由而非生成表达性。在分布漂移下,基于自由的选择器在30个案例中29次胜过XM验证集选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成训练中每个样本仅做单次输出比较,导致模型倾向于收敛到单一常见答案而非保留多样行为。Explorative Modeling (XM)提出生成K个输出并只更新最接近者,声称"探索"是与表达性相关的第三预训练轴。但本文认为该轴实为"自由",即行为约束的弱化程度,且需澄清XM的真实优化目标。

Method: 理论证明:证明平均XM损失取决于候选输出错过可接受区域的概率,探索将该概率提升至K次幂;进而论证K>1时命中概率随自由单调上升。经验验证:通过Forward XM实验测量增大K对自由的影响;训练XM候选池,将验证集选择与仅读取未标注父上下文的自由选择器对比。

Result: 更大的K增加或饱和了测量得到的自由,且在上下文依赖目标下每个测试值均增加自由。自由选择器在30个案例中29次胜过XM的验证集选择。作者还提及先前工作中自由选择相比MDL在归纳实验中提升110-500%。

Conclusion: 生成表达性只是对自由的一个模式计数代理,丢弃了自由泛化意义所依赖的扩展结构。XM是手段,自由才是目的;在分布漂移下,直接以自由为选择准则可改进XM。

Abstract: In generative training, a model produces an output and is penalised for its difference from an example. With one output per comparison, a model that produces one common answer can outperform a model retaining a broader repertoire. Explorative Modeling (XM) produces $K$ outputs per comparison and updates on the closest, claiming exploration as a "third pretraining axis" associated with generative expressivity. Here I show the third axis is actually freedom, meaning the weakness of the constraint implied by a model's behaviour. Previous work showed freedom is a property of function rather than form. Parameters, architecture, minimum-description-length (MDL), and data can vary while the behavioural constraint remains unchanged. It was formally proved that weakest models are likeliest to generalise, and freedom selection beat MDL by 110-500\% in induction experiments. I prove average XM loss depends on the chance a candidate misses an acceptable region, with exploration raising miss probability to power $K$. For $K>1$, match probability rises with freedom. I then demonstrate empirically that XM optimises for freedom. In a Forward XM experiment, larger $K$ increased or saturated measured freedom, and increased freedom at every tested value under context-dependent targets. I trained XM candidate pools and compared validation selection with a freedom selector that read unlabelled parent contexts. Freedom won in 29 of 30 cases. Generative expressivity is a mode-count proxy for freedom, that discards the extension structure that gives freedom its generalisation significance.
